请先读取术语对应表文件 tasks\unnumberedTask\naming_glossary.md（若无法直接读取，请要求用户提供其内容）。 该文件包含：CamelCase 拆分规则、特殊前缀识别、Is 布尔前缀规则、时间列译法、以及按领域分类的英→日术语对应表（系统共通／交易操作／组织店铺／商品税／值下げ／支払現金／ポイント／BO 等）。 所有译名必须以该对应表为准，禁止自由音译或直译。

任务说明

tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup 中有一个文件夹structMaster，请你对structMaster文件夹中的文件进行处理，并将处理后的文件保存到tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup的另一个文件夹中(新建一个)。

需要处理的这些文件是 CSV 格式的数据库表定义： "カラム名","型","NULL","PK","説明"

请只填充最后一列「説明」的日语说明，其余列原样保留，输出完整 CSV（含表头）。

处理流程

  1. 拆分驼峰命名：按 naming_glossary.md 第 0.1 节规则，将「カラム名」按大写字母边界拆成词素。连续大写（IP、EJ、RM、MD 等）视为整体，勿拆散。
  2. 识别特殊前缀：按第 0.2 节处理 M(中間)、BO(保留)、E(保留) 前缀；按第 0.3 节处理 Is 布尔前缀。
  3. 逐词素查表翻译：
  4. 缩略词（No/Qty/Desc 等）→ 第 9 节还原后翻译
  5. 英文缩略语（IP/ID/URL 等）→ 第 10 节保留大写英文
  6. 其余词素 → 按所属领域在对应小节查表（第 1~8 节）
  7. Type 必须按第 1 节「三通り」规则，根据语境在「種別／区分／タイプ」中选择
  8. Discount 系必须按第 5 节三义分立（値下げ／値引／割引）
  9. 直接拼接：按第 0.4 节，词素按原顺序无缝拼接，不加连接词或助词。
  10. 时间列特判：纯日期(date)→日付，含时间(datetime2/timestamp)→日時，营业日→営業日，时间段→時間帯。

输出规则

  1. 保留全部原始列，仅修改「説明」一列，输出完整 CSV（含表头）。
  2. 格式严格一致：字段用双引号包裹，逗号分隔，与输入完全相同。
  3. 译名贴合日本零售 POS（業務系システム）习惯，简短自然。
  4. 表中未列出的词，给出最符合 POS 业务的译法，不留空；若与表中已有近义词冲突，优先服从已收录实例。
  5. 型为 int 且疑似枚举/状态码 → 译「〇〇状態/〇〇区分/〇〇種別」，勿草率译「タイプ」（设备类型除外）。

Requirements for Outputs

All Excel files

Professional Font

  • Use a consistent, professional font (e.g., Arial, Times New Roman) for all deliverables unless otherwise instructed by the user

Zero Formula Errors

  • Every Excel model MUST be delivered with ZERO formula errors (#REF!, #DIV/0!, #VALUE!, #N/A, #NAME?)

Preserve Existing Templates (when updating templates)

  • Study and EXACTLY match existing format, style, and conventions when modifying files
  • Never impose standardized formatting on files with established patterns
  • Existing template conventions ALWAYS override these guidelines

Financial models

Color Coding Standards

Unless otherwise stated by the user or existing template

Industry-Standard Color Conventions

  • Blue text (RGB: 0,0,255): Hardcoded inputs, and numbers users will change for scenarios
  • Black text (RGB: 0,0,0): ALL formulas and calculations
  • Green text (RGB: 0,128,0): Links pulling from other worksheets within same workbook
  • Red text (RGB: 255,0,0): External links to other files
  • Yellow background (RGB: 255,255,0): Key assumptions needing attention or cells that need to be updated

Number Formatting Standards

Required Format Rules

  • Years: Format as text strings (e.g., "2024" not "2,024")
  • Currency: Use $#,##0 format; ALWAYS specify units in headers ("Revenue ($mm)")
  • Zeros: Use number formatting to make all zeros "-", including percentages (e.g., "$#,##0;($#,##0);-")
  • Percentages: Default to 0.0% format (one decimal)
  • Multiples: Format as 0.0x for valuation multiples (EV/EBITDA, P/E)
  • Negative numbers: Use parentheses (123) not minus -123

Formula Construction Rules

Assumptions Placement

  • Place ALL assumptions (growth rates, margins, multiples, etc.) in separate assumption cells
  • Use cell references instead of hardcoded values in formulas
  • Example: Use =B5(1+$B) instead of =B51.05

Formula Error Prevention

  • Verify all cell references are correct
  • Check for off-by-one errors in ranges
  • Ensure consistent formulas across all projection periods
  • Test with edge cases (zero values, negative numbers)
  • Verify no unintended circular references

Documentation Requirements for Hardcodes

  • Comment or in cells beside (if end of table). Format: "Source: [System/Document], [Date], [Specific Reference], [URL if applicable]"
  • Examples:
  • "Source: Company 10-K, FY2024, Page 45, Revenue Note, [SEC EDGAR URL]"
  • "Source: Company 10-Q, Q2 2025, Exhibit 99.1, [SEC EDGAR URL]"
  • "Source: Bloomberg Terminal, 8/15/2025, AAPL US Equity"
  • "Source: FactSet, 8/20/2025, Consensus Estimates Screen"

XLSX creation, editing, and analysis

Overview

A user may ask you to create, edit, or analyze the contents of an .xlsx file. You have different tools and workflows available for different tasks.

Important Requirements

LibreOffice Required for Formula Recalculation: You can assume LibreOffice is installed for recalculating formula values using the scripts/recalc.py script. The script automatically configures LibreOffice on first run, including in sandboxed environments where Unix sockets are restricted (handled by scripts/office/soffice.py)

Reading and analyzing data

Data analysis with pandas

For data analysis, visualization, and basic operations, use pandas which provides powerful data manipulation capabilities:

import pandas as pd

# Read Excel
df = pd.read_excel('file.xlsx')  # Default: first sheet
all_sheets = pd.read_excel('file.xlsx', sheet_name=None)  # All sheets as dict

# Analyze
df.head()      # Preview data
df.info()      # Column info
df.describe()  # Statistics

# Write Excel
df.to_excel('output.xlsx', index=False)

Excel File Workflows

CRITICAL: Use Formulas, Not Hardcoded Values

Always use Excel formulas instead of calculating values in Python and hardcoding them. This ensures the spreadsheet remains dynamic and updateable.

❌ WRONG - Hardcoding Calculated Values

# Bad: Calculating in Python and hardcoding result
total = df['Sales'].sum()
sheet['B10'] = total  # Hardcodes 5000

# Bad: Computing growth rate in Python
growth = (df.iloc[-1]['Revenue'] - df.iloc[0]['Revenue']) / df.iloc[0]['Revenue']
sheet['C5'] = growth  # Hardcodes 0.15

# Bad: Python calculation for average
avg = sum(values) / len(values)
sheet['D20'] = avg  # Hardcodes 42.5

✅ CORRECT - Using Excel Formulas

# Good: Let Excel calculate the sum
sheet['B10'] = '=SUM(B2:B9)'

# Good: Growth rate as Excel formula
sheet['C5'] = '=(C4-C2)/C2'

# Good: Average using Excel function
sheet['D20'] = '=AVERAGE(D2:D19)'

This applies to ALL calculations - totals, percentages, ratios, differences, etc. The spreadsheet should be able to recalculate when source data changes.

Common Workflow

  1. Choose tool: pandas for data, openpyxl for formulas/formatting
  2. Create/Load: Create new workbook or load existing file
  3. Modify: Add/edit data, formulas, and formatting
  4. Save: Write to file
  5. Recalculate formulas (MANDATORY IF USING FORMULAS): Use the scripts/recalc.py script bash python scripts/recalc.py output.xlsx
  6. Verify and fix any errors:
  7. The script returns JSON with error details
  8. If status is errors_found, check error_summary for specific error types and locations
  9. Fix the identified errors and recalculate again
  10. Common errors to fix:
    • #REF!: Invalid cell references
    • #DIV/0!: Division by zero
    • #VALUE!: Wrong data type in formula
    • #NAME?: Unrecognized formula name

Creating new Excel files

# Using openpyxl for formulas and formatting
from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.styles import Font, PatternFill, Alignment

wb = Workbook()
sheet = wb.active

# Add data
sheet['A1'] = 'Hello'
sheet['B1'] = 'World'
sheet.append(['Row', 'of', 'data'])

# Add formula
sheet['B2'] = '=SUM(A1:A10)'

# Formatting
sheet['A1'].font = Font(bold=True, color='FF0000')
sheet['A1'].fill = PatternFill('solid', start_color='FFFF00')
sheet['A1'].alignment = Alignment(horizontal='center')

# Column width
sheet.column_dimensions['A'].width = 20

wb.save('output.xlsx')

Editing existing Excel files

# Using openpyxl to preserve formulas and formatting
from openpyxl import load_workbook

# Load existing file
wb = load_workbook('existing.xlsx')
sheet = wb.active  # or wb['SheetName'] for specific sheet

# Working with multiple sheets
for sheet_name in wb.sheetnames:
    sheet = wb[sheet_name]
    print(f"Sheet: {sheet_name}")

# Modify cells
sheet['A1'] = 'New Value'
sheet.insert_rows(2)  # Insert row at position 2
sheet.delete_cols(3)  # Delete column 3

# Add new sheet
new_sheet = wb.create_sheet('NewSheet')
new_sheet['A1'] = 'Data'

wb.save('modified.xlsx')

Recalculating formulas

Excel files created or modified by openpyxl contain formulas as strings but not calculated values. Use the provided scripts/recalc.py script to recalculate formulas:

python scripts/recalc.py <excel_file> [timeout_seconds]

Example:

python scripts/recalc.py output.xlsx 30

The script: - Automatically sets up LibreOffice macro on first run - Recalculates all formulas in all sheets - Scans ALL cells for Excel errors (#REF!, #DIV/0!, etc.) - Returns JSON with detailed error locations and counts - Works on both Linux and macOS

Formula Verification Checklist

Quick checks to ensure formulas work correctly:

Essential Verification

  • [ ] Test 2-3 sample references: Verify they pull correct values before building full model
  • [ ] Column mapping: Confirm Excel columns match (e.g., column 64 = BL, not BK)
  • [ ] Row offset: Remember Excel rows are 1-indexed (DataFrame row 5 = Excel row 6)

Common Pitfalls

  • [ ] NaN handling: Check for null values with pd.notna()
  • [ ] Far-right columns: FY data often in columns 50+
  • [ ] Multiple matches: Search all occurrences, not just first
  • [ ] Division by zero: Check denominators before using / in formulas (#DIV/0!)
  • [ ] Wrong references: Verify all cell references point to intended cells (#REF!)
  • [ ] Cross-sheet references: Use correct format (Sheet1!A1) for linking sheets

Formula Testing Strategy

  • [ ] Start small: Test formulas on 2-3 cells before applying broadly
  • [ ] Verify dependencies: Check all cells referenced in formulas exist
  • [ ] Test edge cases: Include zero, negative, and very large values

Interpreting scripts/recalc.py Output

The script returns JSON with error details:

{
  "status": "success",           // or "errors_found"
  "total_errors": 0,              // Total error count
  "total_formulas": 42,           // Number of formulas in file
  "error_summary": {              // Only present if errors found
    "#REF!": {
      "count": 2,
      "locations": ["Sheet1!B5", "Sheet1!C10"]
    }
  }
}

Best Practices

Library Selection

  • pandas: Best for data analysis, bulk operations, and simple data export
  • openpyxl: Best for complex formatting, formulas, and Excel-specific features

Working with openpyxl

  • Cell indices are 1-based (row=1, column=1 refers to cell A1)
  • Use data_only=True to read calculated values: load_workbook('file.xlsx', data_only=True)
  • Warning: If opened with data_only=True and saved, formulas are replaced with values and permanently lost
  • For large files: Use read_only=True for reading or write_only=True for writing
  • Formulas are preserved but not evaluated - use scripts/recalc.py to update values

Working with pandas

  • Specify data types to avoid inference issues: pd.read_excel('file.xlsx', dtype={'id': str})
  • For large files, read specific columns: pd.read_excel('file.xlsx', usecols=['A', 'C', 'E'])
  • Handle dates properly: pd.read_excel('file.xlsx', parse_dates=['date_column'])

Code Style Guidelines

IMPORTANT: When generating Python code for Excel operations: - Write minimal, concise Python code without unnecessary comments - Avoid verbose variable names and redundant operations - Avoid unnecessary print statements

For Excel files themselves: - Add comments to cells with complex formulas or important assumptions - Document data sources for hardcoded values - Include notes for key calculations and model sections

1	# POS4U フィールド名 → 日本語用語集（命名対応表）
2	
3	> 本ファイルは、POS4U データベースのカラム名／ストアド名に日本語説明を付与する際の**用語対応表**である。
4	> 各種仕様書（`01_master_db_tables.md`、`02_tran_db_tables.md`、`03_enum_flag_reference*.md`、`04_stored_procedures.md`、`05_er_diagram.md` 等）から抽出した**実例ベースの訳語**を掲載している。
5	> 自由音訳・直訳は禁止し、原則として本表の訳語に従うこと。
6	
7	---
8	
9	## 0. 前処理ルール
10	
11	### 0.1 CamelCase の分割
12	- 大文字を境界に単語（形態素）ごとに分割する。
13	  - `LastUpdateDate` → Last + Update + Date
14	  - `ChangeReserveTotal` → Change + Reserve + Total
15	- 連続大文字は一つの略語として扱い、分割しない。
16	  - `Ip→IP`、`Id→ID`、`Url→URL`、`EJ→EJ`、`RM→RM`、`EMoney→E+Money`、`MD→MD`、`ABC→ABC`、`OTC→OTC`、`VM→VM`、`MSR→MSR`、`CAFIS→CAFIS`
17	
18	### 0.2 特殊プレフィックスの識別
19	| プレフィックス | 意味 | 例 |
20	|----------------|------|----|
21	| `M` | 中間 | MTransaction→中間取引、MTransactionId→中間取引ID、MTran→中間トラン |
22	| `BO` | バックオフィス（BO のまま残す） | BOUser→BOユーザー、BOSession→BOセッション |
23	| `E` | E のまま残す | EJournal→EJournal、ECoupon→Eクーポン |
24	
25	### 0.3 `Is` プレフィックス（bool フラグ）の訳し方
26	- `Is` + 動作/状態 → 「〇〇フラグ」または「〇〇状態／〇〇完了」
27	- `IsXxxProhibition` → 「〇〇禁止フラグ」
28	- `IsXxxTarget` → 「〇〇対象フラグ」
29	- 例：`IsLocked`→ロックフラグ、`IsOpenCount`→開設完了フラグ、`IsCloseCount`→精算完了フラグ、`IsItemManualDiscountProhibition`→手動値引き禁止フラグ、`IsRevenueStampTarget`→収入印紙対象フラグ
30	
31	### 0.4 結合
32	- 形態素を元の順序で**そのまま結合**する。接続詞・助詞は入れない。
33	  - `OfflineIpAddress` → オフラインIPアドレス
34	  - `HeadquartersTransferState` → 本部転送状態
35	  - `ChangeReserveTotal` → 釣銭準備金計
36	
37	---
38	
39	## 1. システム／DB 共通用語 ★直訳しやすい高頻度語★
40	
41	| 英語 | 日本語 | 備考 |
42	|------|--------|------|
43	| Master | マスタ | **長音符なし**（マスター×） |
44	| Management / Manager | 管理 | |
45	| State | 状態 | |
46	| Status | ステータス | |
47	| Flag | フラグ | |
48	| Code | コード | |
49	| Name | 名／名称 | |
50	| Value | 値 | |
51	| Key | キー | |
52	| Version | バージョン | Major/Minor も同様 |
53	| Uri | URI | |

55	| Count | カウント／件数 | BusinessCount→営業カウント |
56	| Total | 計 | SalesTotal→売上計、CashTotal→現金計。集計は「合計」 |
57	| Sum | 合計 | |

61	| Range | 帯 | PriceRange→価格帯 |

63	### Type の三通り（文脈で選択）
64	| 英語 | 日本語 | 例 |
65	|------|--------|----|
66	| Type（業務分類） | **種別** | NodeType→端末種別、PauseType→保留種別、OrderType→注文種別 |
67	| Type（区分・性質） | **区分** | TaxType→税区分、DepartmentType→部門区分、DiscountType→値下げ種別 |
68	| Type（機器・技術） | **タイプ** | TerminalType→端末タイプ、PointType→ポイントタイプ、StoreType→店舗タイプ |
69	
70	### 時間系

Assistant2026-07-01T00:56:14.868Z
$ Bash
Extract all unique column names across CSV files
cd "D:\POJ\NoteType\tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup\structMaster" && python -c "
import csv, glob, os
from collections import defaultdict
col_to_files = defaultdict(list)
total_rows = 0
for f in sorted(glob.glob('*.csv')):
    with open(f, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
        r = csv.reader(fh)
        rows = list(r)
        for row in rows[1:]:
            if row:
                total_rows += 1
                col_to_files[row[0]].append(os.path.splitext(f)[0])
print('TOTAL data rows:', total_rows)
print('UNIQUE column names:', len(col_to_files))
print('==== ALL UNIQUE COLUMN NAMES (sorted) ====')
for c in sorted(col_to_files, key=lambda x: x.lower()):
    print(c)
"

Assistant2026-07-01T00:57:13.241Z
$ Bash
Extract columns with file context and type
cd "D:\POJ\NoteType\tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup\structMaster" && python -c "
import csv, glob, os
# column -> list of (file, type)
from collections import defaultdict
info = defaultdict(list)
for f in sorted(glob.glob('*.csv')):
    tbl = os.path.splitext(f)[0]
    with open(f, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
        for row in list(csv.reader(fh))[1:]:
            if row and len(row) >= 4:
                info[row[0]].append((tbl, row[1]))
for c in sorted(info, key=lambda x: x.lower()):
    files = ', '.join(sorted(set(t for t,_ in info[c])))
    types = ', '.join(sorted(set(t for _,t in info[c])))
    print(f'{c}\t[{types}]\t{files}')
" > /tmp/cols_context.txt 2>&1; wc -l /tmp/cols_context.txt

Print columns with file context and type
cd "D:\POJ\NoteType\tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup\structMaster" && python -c "
import csv, glob, os
from collections import defaultdict
info = defaultdict(list)
for f in sorted(glob.glob('*.csv')):
    tbl = os.path.splitext(f)[0]
    with open(f, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
        for row in list(csv.reader(fh))[1:]:
            if row and len(row) >= 4:
                info[row[0]].append((tbl, row[1]))
for c in sorted(info, key=lambda x: x.lower()):
    files = ', '.join(sorted(set(t for t,_ in info[c])))
    types = ', '.join(sorted(set(t for _,t in info[c])))

def translate(col):
    if col in T:
        return T[col]
    # フォールバック（未定義カラムがあれば警告して空にはしない）
    print("WARNING: no translation for:", col, file=sys.stderr)
    return col


def main():
    os.makedirs(DST, exist_ok=True)
    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(SRC, "*.csv")))
    total_rows = 0
    for f in files:
        with open(f, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
            rows = list(csv.reader(fh))
        out_name = os.path.basename(f)
        with open(os.path.join(DST, out_name), "w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as fh:
            w = csv.writer(fh, quoting=csv.QUOTE_ALL, lineterminator="\r\n")
            for i, row in enumerate(rows):
                if i == 0:
                    # ヘッダ行はそのまま
                    w.writerow(row)
                elif row:
                    # 説明列(5列目, idx=4)のみ書き換え
                    col = row[0]
                    new_row = list(row)
                    while len(new_row) < 5:
                        new_row.append("")
                    new_row[4] = translate(col)
                    w.writerow(new_row[:5])
                    total_rows += 1
    print("files:", len(files), "rows:", total_rows)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import glob, os, csv
src=sorted(glob.glob('structMaster/*.csv'))
dst=sorted(glob.glob('structMaster_ja/*.csv'))
print('src files:',len(src),' dst files:',len(dst))
assert len(src)==len(dst)
problems=0
empty=0
for s,d in zip(src,dst):
    # BOM + CRLF checks
    rb=open(s,'rb').read(); db=open(d,'rb').read()
    bom = db[:3]==b'\xef\xbb\xbf'
    crlf = b'\r\n' in db and not b'\n\r' in db and db.replace(b'\r\n',b'').find(b'\n')==-1
    trail = db.endswith(b'\r\n')
    if not(bom and crlf and trail):
        print('FORMAT ISSUE',d,'bom=',bom,'crlf=',crlf,'trail=',trail); problems+=1
    # row/col checks
    sr=list(csv.reader(open(s,encoding='utf-8-sig')))
    dr=list(csv.reader(open(d,encoding='utf-8-sig')))
    if len(sr)!=len(dr):
        print('ROWCOUNT MISMATCH',d,len(sr),len(dr)); problems+=1
    for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(sr,dr)):
        if len(b)!=5: print('COLCOUNT',d,i,len(b)); problems+=1
        # first 4 cols identical
        if a[:4]!=b[:4]: print('COLCHANGE',d,i,a[:4],b[:4]); problems+=1
        if i>0 and b[4]=='': empty+=1; print('EMPTY',d,b[0])
print('format problems:',problems,' empty 説明:',empty)
print('header dst:', list(csv.reader(open(dst[0],encoding='utf-8-sig')))[0])

用語集ルールの適用状況

  • Type 三通り: NodeType→端末種別、TaxType→税区分、TerminalType→端末タイプ、PrintType→印字区分 … 文脈で使い分け。
  • Discount 三義: DiscountType→値下げ種別、IsItemPercentOffProhibition→商品割引禁止フラグ、IsItemManualDiscountProhibition→手動値引き禁止フラグ（用語集实例どおり）。
  • Is 接頭辞: 全件「〇〇フラグ／〇〇禁止フラグ／〇〇対象フラグ」化。
  • 時間列特判: datetime2→日時、date→日付、BusinessDate→営業日、LastUpdateDate→最終更新日時。

判断を要した箇所（要レビュー推奨）

用語集／仕様書に記載がなく、POS 慣行で判断したものです：

カラム 訳 判断理由
BillNECount* / CoinNFCount* 1000円札NE枚数 等 NE/NF は釣銭機の装置固有ラベル。仕様不明のため IP/ID 同様大文字保持＋金額・枚数は明示
EJournal* Eジャーナル* 用語集の E→Eクーポン パターンに準拠（EJournal→EJournal 表記は E 接頭辞保持の意と解釈）
*Sticks 〇円玉束数 硬貨の束（バンド）＝束
TenThousandCount 等 10000円札枚数 / 500円玉枚数 紙幣=札、硬貨=玉
DifferenceCash 差分現金 ルール 0.4（語順保持）に従い、自然な「現金差分」ではなく原順
Volume 数量 numeric 整数のため容積ではなく数量と判断

翻訳元の辞書スクリプトは tasks\unnumberedTask\databaseCleanup\_translate.py に残しています（再実行や訳語調整に使えます。不要なら削除してください）。用語集にない語の訳で修正したいものがあれば、同スクリプトの辞書 T を編集して再実行すれば全ファイルへ一括反映されます。
